Raw (uninitialized) storage lets the first write to\n    // every page come from the thread that will later work on that row range,\n    // so the pages end up on the right NUMA node.\n    const size_t elems = numNodes * numNodes;\n    const size_t bytes = ((elems * sizeof(unsigned int)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    auto deleter = [](unsigned int* p) { std::free(p); We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // (package, core) -> its logical CPUs, in increasing order.\n    std::map<std::pair<int, int>, std::vector<int>> cores;\n    for (const int cpu : cpus) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            return cpus;  // no usable topology info, keep the natural order\n        }\n        cores[{pkg, core}].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    // Cores grouped per package, packages in increasing id order.\n    std::map<int, std::vector<const std::vector<int>*>> byPackage;\n    for (const auto& entry : cores) {\n        byPackage[entry.first.first].push_back(&entry.second);\n    }\n    const size_t numPackages = byPackage.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numThreads);\n    size_t pkgIndex = 0;\n    for (const auto& pkg : byPackage) {\n        // Even split of the threads over the packages.\n        const size_t share = numThreads / numPackages +\n                             (pkgIndex < numThreads % numPackages ? 1 : 0);\n        ++pkgIndex;\n        size_t placed = 0;\n        for (size_t smt = 0;  // respect the placement the user asked for\n    }\n    static const std::vector<int> order = buildCpuOrder();\n    if (order.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[(size_t)omp_get_thread_num() % order.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a\n// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements.\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;\n            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;\n        }\n        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax the block of destination rows [i0,i1) x columns [j0,j1) against all\n// intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). This is exactly the body of the classic\n// triple loop restricted to a sub-range of (k, i, j).\nstatic inline void relaxBlock(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                              const size_t n,\n                              const size_t k0, const size_t k1,\n                              const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                              const size_t j0, const size_t j1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = dist + k * n;\n        for (size_t i = i0; i < i1; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict rowI = dist + i * n;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * n;\n            const unsigned int distIK = rowI[k];\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = j0; j < j1; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ = rowI[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                rowI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? (unsigned int)k : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pick a tile size that keeps the three active tiles resident in cache while\n// still exposing enough independent tiles to keep every thread busy.\nstatic size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t numNodes, const int numThreads) {\n    if (const char* e = getenv(\"FW_B\")) { return std::min((size_t)atoi(e), std::max<size_t>(numNodes,1)); }\n    const size_t candidates[] = {128, 96, 64, 48, 32};\n    const size_t minTiles = 2 * (size_t)numThreads;\n    size_t chosen = candidates[sizeof(candidates) / sizeof(candidates[0]) - 1];\n    for (const size_t b : candidates) {\n        const size_t nb = (numNodes + b - 1) / b;\n        if (nb * nb >= minTiles) {\n            chosen = b;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return std::min(chosen, std::max<size_t>(numNodes, 1));\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t B = chooseBlockSize(numNodes, numThreads);\n    const size_t nb = (numNodes + B - 1) / B;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < nb; ++kb) {\n            const size_t k0 = kb * B;\n            const size_t k1 = std::min(k0 + B, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the pivot tile depends only on itself and must be\n            // relaxed sequentially in increasing k.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 2: the pivot row-panel and column-panel of tiles; ib < nb; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nb; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t i0 = ib * B;\n                    const size_t i1 = std::min(i0 + B, numNodes);\n                    const size_t j0 = jb * B;\n                    const size_t j1 = std::min(j0 + B, numNodes);\n                    relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, i0, i1, j0, j1);\n                }\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    // 1. We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // (package, core) -> its logical CPUs, in increasing order.\n    std::map<std::pair<int, int>, std::vector<int>> cores;\n    for (const int cpu : cpus) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            return cpus;  // no usable topology info, keep the natural order\n        }\n        cores[{pkg, core}].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    // Cores grouped per package, packages in increasing id order.\n    std::map<int, std::vector<const std::vector<int>*>> byPackage;\n    for (const auto& entry : cores) {\n        byPackage[entry.first.first].push_back(&entry.second);\n    }\n    const size_t numPackages = byPackage.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numThreads);\n    size_t pkgIndex = 0;\n    for (const auto& pkg : byPackage) {\n        // Even split of the threads over the packages.\n        const size_t share = numThreads / numPackages +\n                             (pkgIndex < numThreads % numPackages ? 1 : 0);\n        ++pkgIndex;\n\n        // CPUs of this package, all first SMT threads before all second ones.\n        std::vector<int> pkgCpus;\n        for (size_t smt = 0;; ++smt) {\n            bool any = false;\n            for (const std::vector<int>* core : pkg.second) {\n                if (smt < core->size()) {\n                    pkgCpus.push_back((*core)[smt]);\n                    any = true;\n                }\n            }\n            if (!any) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < share && !pkgCpus.empty(); ++t) {\n            order.push_back(pkgCpus[t % pkgCpus.size()]);\n        }\n    }\n    return order.empty() ? cpus : order;\n}\n\nstatic void bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;  // respect the placement the user asked for\n    }\n    const std::vector<int> order = buildCpuOrder((size_t)omp_get_max_threads());\n    if (order.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[(size_t)omp_get_thread_num() % order.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a\n// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements.\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;\n            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;\n        }\n        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax the block of destination rows [i0,i1) x columns [j0,j1) against all\n// intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). This is exactly the body of the classic\n// triple loop restricted to a sub-range of (k, i, j).\nstatic inline void relaxBlock(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                              const size_t n,\n                              const size_t k0, const size_t k1,\n                              const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                              const size_t j0, const size_t j1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = dist + k * n;\n        for (size_t i = i0; i < i1; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict rowI = dist + i * n;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * n;\n            const unsigned int distIK = rowI[k];\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = j0; j < j1; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ = rowI[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                rowI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? (unsigned int)k : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pick a tile size that keeps the three active tiles resident in cache while\n// still exposing enough independent tiles to keep every thread busy.\nstatic size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t numNodes, const int numThreads) {\n    if (const char* e = getenv(\"FW_B\")) { return std::min((size_t)atoi(e), std::max<size_t>(numNodes,1)); }\n    const size_t candidates[] = {128, 96, 64, 48, 32};\n    const size_t minTiles = 2 * (size_t)numThreads;\n    size_t chosen = candidates[sizeof(candidates) / sizeof(candidates[0]) - 1];\n    for (const size_t b : candidates) {\n        const size_t nb = (numNodes + b - 1) / b;\n        if (nb * nb >= minTiles) {\n            chosen = b;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return std::min(chosen, std::max<size_t>(numNodes, 1));\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t B = chooseBlockSize(numNodes, numThreads);\n    const size_t nb = (numNodes + B - 1) / B;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < nb; ++kb) {\n            const size_t k0 = kb * B;\n            const size_t k1 = std::min(k0 + B, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the pivot tile depends only on itself and must be\n            // relaxed sequentially in increasing k.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 2: the pivot row-panel and column-panel of tiles; each of\n            // them depends on the pivot tile only, so they are independent.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t b = 0; b < nb; ++b) {\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t o0 = b * B;\n                const size_t o1 = std::min(o0 + B, numNodes);\n                // Row panel: rows [k0,k1), columns [o0,o1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, o0, o1);\n                // Column panel: rows [o0,o1), columns [k0,k1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, o0, o1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 3: every remaining tile depends only on the two panels.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < nb; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nb; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t i0 = ib * B;\n                    const size_t i1 = std::min(i0 + B, numNodes);\n                    const size_t j0 = jb * B;\n                    const size_t j1 = std::min(j0 + B, numNodes);\n                    relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, i0, i1, j0, j1);\n                }\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    if (!valid) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t limit = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Allocate matrices. Raw (uninitialized) storage lets the first write to\n    // every page come from the thread that will later work on that row range,\n    // so the pages end up on the right NUMA node.\n    const size_t elems = numNodes * numNodes;\n    const size_t bytes = ((elems * sizeof(unsigned int)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    auto deleter = [](unsigned int* p) { std::free(p); We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // (package, core) -> its logical CPUs, in increasing order.\n    std::map<std::pair<int, int>, std::vector<int>> cores;\n    for (const int cpu : cpus) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            return cpus;  // no usable topology info, keep the natural order\n        }\n        cores[{pkg, core}].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    // Cores grouped per package, packages in increasing id order.\n    std::map<int, std::vector<const std::vector<int>*>> byPackage;\n    for (const auto& entry : cores) {\n        byPackage[entry.first.first].push_back(&entry.second);\n    }\n    const size_t numPackages = byPackage.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numThreads);\n    size_t pkgIndex = 0;\n    for (const auto& pkg : byPackage) {\n        // Even split of the threads over the packages.\n        const size_t share = numThreads / numPackages +\n                             (pkgIndex < numThreads % numPackages ? 1 : 0);\n        ++pkgIndex;\n\n        // Walk the package's cores at a constant stride: with fewer threads\n        // than cores this spreads them over all L3 slices, and with more it\n        // gives consecutive thread ids to the SMT siblings of one core, so\n        // that a static schedule hands neighbouring work to a shared cache.\n        const size_t numCores = pkg.second.size();\n        if (numCores == 0) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        size_t previousCore = (size_t)-1;\n        size_t onThisCore = 0;\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < share; ++t) {\n            const size_t c = t * numCores / share;\n            if (c != previousCore) {\n                previousCore = c;\n                onThisCore = 0;\n            }\n            const std::vector<int>& coreCpus = *pkg.second[c];\n            order.push_back(coreCpus[onThisCore % coreCpus.size()]);\n            ++onThisCore;\n        }\n    }\n    return order.empty() ? cpus : order;\n}\n\nstatic void bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;  // respect the placement the user asked for\n    }\n    const std::vector<int> order = buildCpuOrder((size_t)omp_get_max_threads());\n    if (order.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[(size_t)omp_get_thread_num() % order.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a\n// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements.\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;\n            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;\n        }\n        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax the block of destination rows [i0,i1) x columns [j0,j1) against all\n// intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). This is exactly the body of the classic\n// triple loop restricted to a sub-range of (k, i, j).\nstatic inline void relaxBlock(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                              const size_t n,\n                              const size_t k0, const size_t k1,\n                              const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                              const size_t j0, const size_t j1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = dist + k * n;\n        for (size_t i = i0; i < i1; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict rowI = dist + i * n;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * n;\n            const unsigned int distIK = rowI[k];\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = j0; j < j1; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ = rowI[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                rowI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? (unsigned int)k : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pick a tile size that keeps the three active tiles resident in cache while\n// still exposing enough independent tiles to keep every thread busy.\nstatic size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t numNodes, const int numThreads) {\n    if (const char* e = getenv(\"FW_B\")) { return std::min((size_t)atoi(e), std::max<size_t>(numNodes,1)); }\n    const size_t candidates[] = {128, 96, 64, 48, 32};\n    const size_t minTiles = 2 * (size_t)numThreads;\n    size_t chosen = candidates[sizeof(candidates) / sizeof(candidates[0]) - 1];\n    for (const size_t b : candidates) {\n        const size_t nb = (numNodes + b - 1) / b;\n        if (nb * nb >= minTiles) {\n            chosen = b;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return std::min(chosen, std::max<size_t>(numNodes, 1));\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t B = chooseBlockSize(numNodes, numThreads);\n    const size_t nb = (numNodes + B - 1) / B;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < nb; ++kb) {\n            const size_t k0 = kb * B;\n            const size_t k1 = std::min(k0 + B, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the pivot tile depends only on itself and must be\n            // relaxed sequentially in increasing k.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 2: the pivot row-panel and column-panel of tiles; each of\n            // them depends on the pivot tile only, so they are independent.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t b = 0; b < nb; ++b) {\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t o0 = b * B;\n                const size_t o1 = std::min(o0 + B, numNodes);\n                // Row panel: rows [k0,k1), columns [o0,o1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, o0, o1);\n                // Column panel: rows [o0,o1), columns [k0,k1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, o0, o1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 3: every remaining tile depends only on the two panels.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < nb; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nb; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t i0 = ib * B;\n                    const size_t i1 = std::min(i0 + B, numNodes);\n                    const size_t j0 = jb * B;\n                    const size_t j1 = std::min(j0 + B, numNodes);\n                    relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, i0, i1, j0, j1);\n                }\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    if (!valid) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t limit = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < limit; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < limit; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        valid = false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Allocate matrices. Raw (uninitialized) storage lets the first write to\n    // every page come from the thread that will later work on that row range,\n    // so the pages end up on the right NUMA node.\n    const size_t elems = numNodes * numNodes;\n    const size_t bytes = ((elems * sizeof(unsigned int)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    auto deleter = [](unsigned int* p) { std::free(p); };\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> distStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> pathStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    if (!distStore || !pathStore) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    unsigned int* const dist = distStore.get();\n    unsigned int* const path = pathStore.get();\n\n    // NUMA first touch, distributed exactly like the row blocks of the solver.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numNodes; ++r) {\n        std::memset(dist + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        std::memset(path + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<unsigned int> distView(dist, dist + elems);\n        print_results_int(distView, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":139,"oldLines":30,"newStart":139,"newLines":46,"lines":[" }"," "," // The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a","-// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements.","+// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements. We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // (package, core) -> its logical CPUs, in increasing order.\n    std::map<std::pair<int, int>, std::vector<int>> cores;\n    for (const int cpu : cpus) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            return cpus;  // no usable topology info, keep the natural order\n        }\n        cores[{pkg, core}].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    // Cores grouped per package, packages in increasing id order.\n    std::map<int, std::vector<const std::vector<int>*>> byPackage;\n    for (const auto& entry : cores) {\n        byPackage[entry.first.first].push_back(&entry.second);\n    }\n    const size_t numPackages = byPackage.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numThreads);\n    size_t pkgIndex = 0;\n    for (const auto& pkg : byPackage) {\n        // Even split of the threads over the packages.\n        const size_t share = numThreads / numPackages +\n                             (pkgIndex < numThreads % numPackages ? 1 : 0);\n        ++pkgIndex;\n\n        // Walk the package's cores at a constant stride: with fewer threads\n        // than cores this spreads them over all L3 slices, and with more it\n        // gives consecutive thread ids to the SMT siblings of one core, so\n        // that a static schedule hands neighbouring work to a shared cache.\n        const size_t numCores = pkg.second.size();\n        if (numCores == 0) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        size_t previousCore = (size_t)-1;\n        size_t onThisCore = 0;\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < share; ++t) {\n            const size_t c = t * numCores / share;\n            if (c != previousCore) {\n                previousCore = c;\n                onThisCore = 0;\n            }\n            const std::vector<int>& coreCpus = *pkg.second[c];\n            order.push_back(coreCpus[onThisCore % coreCpus.size()]);\n            ++onThisCore;\n        }\n    }\n    return order.empty() ? cpus : order;\n}\n\nstatic void bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;  // respect the placement the user asked for\n    }\n    const std::vector<int> order = buildCpuOrder((size_t)omp_get_max_threads());\n    if (order.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[(size_t)omp_get_thread_num() % order.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a\n// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements. The matrices are\n// stored with a padded leading dimension `ld` instead of `n`: a row stride\n// that is a large power of two (which is what n * sizeof(unsigned int) is for\n// the usual benchmark sizes) makes all rows of a tile land in the same cache\n// sets and destroys the blocking. `ld` is passed as the stride argument of\n// idx2, so the index arithmetic itself is unchanged.\n\n// Padded row stride: 64-byte aligned rows, offset by one cache line so that\n// rows of a tile spread over the cache sets.\nstatic size_t paddedStride(const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    return ((numNodes + 15) & ~static_cast<size_t>(15)) + 16;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        // Element i of the logical (unpadded) array lives at row i / numNodes.\n        dist[idx2(i % numNodes, i / numNodes, ld)] =\n            rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, ld)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            path[idx2(i, j, ld)] = j;\n            path[idx2(j, i, ld)] = i;\n        }\n        path[idx2(j, j, ld)] = j;\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax the block of destination rows [i0,i1) x columns [j0,j1) against all\n// intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). This is exactly the body of the classic\n// triple loop restricted to a sub-range of (k, i, j).\nstatic inline void relaxBlock(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                              const size_t n,\n                              const size_t k0, const size_t k1,\n                              const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                              const size_t j0, const size_t j1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = dist + k * n;\n        for (size_t i = i0; i < i1; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict rowI = dist + i * n;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * n;\n            const unsigned int distIK = rowI[k];\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = j0; j < j1; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ = rowI[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                rowI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? (unsigned int)k : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pick a tile size that keeps the three active tiles resident in cache while\n// still exposing enough independent tiles to keep every thread busy.\nstatic size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t numNodes, const int numThreads) {\n    if (const char* e = getenv(\"FW_B\")) { return std::min((size_t)atoi(e), std::max<size_t>(numNodes,1)); }\n    const size_t candidates[] = {128, 96, 64, 48, 32};\n    const size_t minTiles = 2 * (size_t)numThreads;\n    size_t chosen = candidates[sizeof(candidates) / sizeof(candidates[0]) - 1];\n    for (const size_t b : candidates) {\n        const size_t nb = (numNodes + b - 1) / b;\n        if (nb * nb >= minTiles) {\n            chosen = b;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return std::min(chosen, std::max<size_t>(numNodes, 1));\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t B = chooseBlockSize(numNodes, numThreads);\n    const size_t nb = (numNodes + B - 1) / B;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < nb; ++kb) {\n            const size_t k0 = kb * B;\n            const size_t k1 = std::min(k0 + B, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the pivot tile depends only on itself and must be\n            // relaxed sequentially in increasing k.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 2: the pivot row-panel and column-panel of tiles; each of\n            // them depends on the pivot tile only, so they are independent.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t b = 0; b < nb; ++b) {\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t o0 = b * B;\n                const size_t o1 = std::min(o0 + B, numNodes);\n                // Row panel: rows [k0,k1), columns [o0,o1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, o0, o1);\n                // Column panel: rows [o0,o1), columns [k0,k1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, o0, o1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 3: every remaining tile depends only on the two panels.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < nb; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nb; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t i0 = ib * B;\n                    const size_t i1 = std::min(i0 + B, numNodes);\n                    const size_t j0 = jb * B;\n                    const size_t j1 = std::min(j0 + B, numNodes);\n                    relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, i0, i1, j0, j1);\n                }\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    if (!valid) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t limit = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < limit; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < limit; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        valid = false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Allocate matrices. Raw (uninitialized) storage lets the first write to\n    // every page come from the thread that will later work on that row range,\n    // so the pages end up on the right NUMA node.\n    const size_t elems = numNodes * numNodes;\n    const size_t bytes = ((elems * sizeof(unsigned int)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    auto deleter = [](unsigned int* p) { std::free(p); };\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> distStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> pathStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    if (!distStore || !pathStore) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    unsigned int* const dist = distStore.get();\n    unsigned int* const path = pathStore.get();\n\n    // NUMA first touch, distributed exactly like the row blocks of the solver.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numNodes; ++r) {\n        std::memset(dist + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        std::memset(path + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<unsigned int> distView(dist, dist + elems);\n        print_results_int(distView, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":160,"oldLines":10,"newStart":160,"newLines":12,"lines":["     unsigned int seed = 42;","     const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;"," ","-    for (size_t i = 0; We therefore pin the threads\n// ourselves, one physical core at a time and round-robin over packages, only\n// falling back to SMT siblings once every core is taken. Any explicit user\n// affinity request wins: we stay inside the inherited CPU mask and do nothing\n// at all if OMP_PROC_BIND / OMP_PLACES / GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY is set.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* what) {\n    char pathBuf[128];\n    snprintf(pathBuf, sizeof(pathBuf), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, what);\n    FILE* f = fopen(pathBuf, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) {\n        value = -1;\n    }\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\n// Build the thread -> CPU map for `numThreads` threads: the threads are split\n// into one contiguous id range per package (so a static loop schedule keeps\n// neighbouring chunks on the same NUMA node), and inside a package they fill\n// distinct physical cores before doubling up on SMT siblings.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder(const size_t numThreads) {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return {};\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // (package, core) -> its logical CPUs, in increasing order.\n    std::map<std::pair<int, int>, std::vector<int>> cores;\n    for (const int cpu : cpus) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            return cpus;  // no usable topology info, keep the natural order\n        }\n        cores[{pkg, core}].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    // Cores grouped per package, packages in increasing id order.\n    std::map<int, std::vector<const std::vector<int>*>> byPackage;\n    for (const auto& entry : cores) {\n        byPackage[entry.first.first].push_back(&entry.second);\n    }\n    const size_t numPackages = byPackage.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(numThreads);\n    size_t pkgIndex = 0;\n    for (const auto& pkg : byPackage) {\n        // Even split of the threads over the packages.\n        const size_t share = numThreads / numPackages +\n                             (pkgIndex < numThreads % numPackages ? 1 : 0);\n        ++pkgIndex;\n\n        // Walk the package's cores at a constant stride: with fewer threads\n        // than cores this spreads them over all L3 slices, and with more it\n        // gives consecutive thread ids to the SMT siblings of one core, so\n        // that a static schedule hands neighbouring work to a shared cache.\n        const size_t numCores = pkg.second.size();\n        if (numCores == 0) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        size_t previousCore = (size_t)-1;\n        size_t onThisCore = 0;\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < share; ++t) {\n            const size_t c = t * numCores / share;\n            if (c != previousCore) {\n                previousCore = c;\n                onThisCore = 0;\n            }\n            const std::vector<int>& coreCpus = *pkg.second[c];\n            order.push_back(coreCpus[onThisCore % coreCpus.size()]);\n            ++onThisCore;\n        }\n    }\n    return order.empty() ? cpus : order;\n}\n\nstatic void bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\")) {\n        return;  // respect the placement the user asked for\n    }\n    const std::vector<int> order = buildCpuOrder((size_t)omp_get_max_threads());\n    if (order.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[(size_t)omp_get_thread_num() % order.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The storage layout above places logical element M[r][c] at r * n + c, i.e. a\n// logical matrix row is a contiguous span of n elements. The matrices are\n// stored with a padded leading dimension `ld` instead of `n`: a row stride\n// that is a large power of two (which is what n * sizeof(unsigned int) is for\n// the usual benchmark sizes) makes all rows of a tile land in the same cache\n// sets and destroys the blocking. `ld` is passed as the stride argument of\n// idx2, so the index arithmetic itself is unchanged.\n\n// Padded row stride: 64-byte aligned rows, offset by one cache line so that\n// rows of a tile spread over the cache sets.\nstatic size_t paddedStride(const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    return ((numNodes + 15) & ~static_cast<size_t>(15)) + 16;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Element i of the logical (unpadded) array is row i / numNodes, column\n    // i % numNodes, so this walks the random sequence in the original order.\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numNodes; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < numNodes; ++c) {\n            dist[idx2(c, r, ld)] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, ld)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes, const size_t ld) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            path[idx2(i, j, ld)] = j;\n            path[idx2(j, i, ld)] = i;\n        }\n        path[idx2(j, j, ld)] = j;\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax the block of destination rows [i0,i1) x columns [j0,j1) against all\n// intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). This is exactly the body of the classic\n// triple loop restricted to a sub-range of (k, i, j).\nstatic inline void relaxBlock(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                              const size_t n,\n                              const size_t k0, const size_t k1,\n                              const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                              const size_t j0, const size_t j1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = dist + k * n;\n        for (size_t i = i0; i < i1; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict rowI = dist + i * n;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * n;\n            const unsigned int distIK = rowI[k];\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = j0; j < j1; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ = rowI[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                rowI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? (unsigned int)k : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pick a tile size that keeps the three active tiles resident in cache while\n// still exposing enough independent tiles to keep every thread busy.\nstatic size_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t numNodes, const int numThreads) {\n    if (const char* e = getenv(\"FW_B\")) { return std::min((size_t)atoi(e), std::max<size_t>(numNodes,1)); }\n    const size_t candidates[] = {128, 96, 64, 48, 32};\n    const size_t minTiles = 2 * (size_t)numThreads;\n    size_t chosen = candidates[sizeof(candidates) / sizeof(candidates[0]) - 1];\n    for (const size_t b : candidates) {\n        const size_t nb = (numNodes + b - 1) / b;\n        if (nb * nb >= minTiles) {\n            chosen = b;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return std::min(chosen, std::max<size_t>(numNodes, 1));\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t B = chooseBlockSize(numNodes, numThreads);\n    const size_t nb = (numNodes + B - 1) / B;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < nb; ++kb) {\n            const size_t k0 = kb * B;\n            const size_t k1 = std::min(k0 + B, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the pivot tile depends only on itself and must be\n            // relaxed sequentially in increasing k.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 2: the pivot row-panel and column-panel of tiles; each of\n            // them depends on the pivot tile only, so they are independent.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t b = 0; b < nb; ++b) {\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t o0 = b * B;\n                const size_t o1 = std::min(o0 + B, numNodes);\n                // Row panel: rows [k0,k1), columns [o0,o1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, k0, k1, o0, o1);\n                // Column panel: rows [o0,o1), columns [k0,k1)\n                relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, o0, o1, k0, k1);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n\n            // Phase 3: every remaining tile depends only on the two panels.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < nb; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nb; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t i0 = ib * B;\n                    const size_t i1 = std::min(i0 + B, numNodes);\n                    const size_t j0 = jb * B;\n                    const size_t j1 = std::min(j0 + B, numNodes);\n                    relaxBlock(dist, path, numNodes, k0, k1, i0, i1, j0, j1);\n                }\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    if (!valid) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t limit = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(&& : valid) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < limit; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < limit; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        valid = false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Allocate matrices. Raw (uninitialized) storage lets the first write to\n    // every page come from the thread that will later work on that row range,\n    // so the pages end up on the right NUMA node.\n    const size_t elems = numNodes * numNodes;\n    const size_t bytes = ((elems * sizeof(unsigned int)) + 63) & ~static_cast<size_t>(63);\n    auto deleter = [](unsigned int* p) { std::free(p); };\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> distStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[], decltype(deleter)> pathStore(\n        static_cast<unsigned int*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes ? bytes : 64)), deleter);\n    if (!distStore || !pathStore) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    unsigned int* const dist = distStore.get();\n    unsigned int* const path = pathStore.get();\n\n    // NUMA first touch, distributed exactly like the row blocks of the solver.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numNodes; ++r) {\n        std::memset(dist + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        std::memset(path + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<unsigned int> distView(dist, dist + elems);\n        print_results_int(distView, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":188,"oldLines":15,"newStart":188,"newLines":15,"lines":[" // intermediate nodes k in [k0,k1). r < numNodes; ++r) {\n        std::memset(dist + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        std::memset(path + r * numNodes, 0, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<unsigned int> distView(dist, dist + elems);\n        print_results_int(distView, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n\n        if (valid) {","numLines":70,"startLine":358,"totalLines":438}}}
